# Skills · N · part 58

888 entries, notes-anki to notification-system. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **notes-anki**: Сгенерировать Anki-карточки из проверенных заметок org-roam — нумерованный список черновиков → пользователь выбирает → карточки пишутся в заметку поддеревом anki-editor под :noexport:. Использовать, когда просят «сделай карточки», «anki по лекции/заметке», /notes-anki [ref|тег|lecture-заметка]. ([TheKostins/roam-notes](https://github.com/TheKostins/roam-notes/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/notes-anki))

- **notes-artifact**: Authors ballot-note prose for one hydrated FHIR artifact unit. USE FOR: resource, profile, terminology, and IG-artifact ballot notes. In outer-control mode it starts and monitors a processor-owned note run, downloads the immutable snapshot, and publishes the review site. In worker mode it reads processor-owned evidence, independently observes CurrentEvidenceRevision, produces one typed BallotNoteProse payload from the after-applied state, submits through the callback, and requires the exact durable receipt. ([GinoCanessa/fhir-augury](https://github.com/GinoCanessa/fhir-augury/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/notes-artifact) · ★2)
- **notes-as-claude-context**: Search and read the user's Apple Notes, contacts and iMessage conversations as context through the local MCP server inside the Exporter Mac app. Use when asked to search my notes, answer from my Apple Notes, find what I wrote about something, look up a conversation or contact, or connect Apple Notes to Claude Code, Claude Desktop, Cursor or VS Code. macOS only. ([systemoperators/claude-plugins](https://github.com/systemoperators/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/exporter/skills/notes-as-claude-context) · ★6)
- **notes-ask**: Handles mid-lesson Q&A when a student encounters doubts, curiosity, or blockers. ([asquilatan/notes-skills](https://github.com/asquilatan/notes-skills/tree/HEAD/notes-ask))
- **notes-audit**: Analyze the macOS Notes folder taxonomy and propose a better organization - find empty/near-empty folders, oversized dumping grounds, duplicate-purpose folders, mixed-language naming, and stale content; then present a target hierarchy for operator approval BEFORE any moves. TRIGGERS - audit my notes, notes are a mess, propose notes organization, analyze notes folders, notes cleanup plan. - **notes_attach_image**: Attach an image file to a Notes note. Notes' AppleScript dictionary does NOT support `make new attachment` for image files, so this skill uses the clipboard-paste path: 1. Read the image into the system pasteboard via `osascript`'s `(read POSIX file "X" as JPEG picture)` coercion (works for JPEG / PNG / TIFF — checks file extension). 2. Activate Notes, select the target note, focus the body. 3. Move cursor to end of body, then Cmd+V — Notes pastes a pasteboard image as an attachment in the note. - **notes_checklist**: Convert a note's body into a Notes-native checklist (HTML `&lt;ul class="gtl-todo-list"&gt;` markup), with optional auto-checking of specific item indices. Agents routinely fail at this by typing markdown `- [ ]` text — Notes does NOT recognize markdown checklist syntax; it stores literal "- [ ]" text. The native checklist requires Cmd+Shift+L on selected text or Format menu → Checklist. This skill: activates Notes, focuses the note body, selects all, Cmd+Shift+L to convert. If `check_indices` is given, navigates to each line and presses Cmd+Shift+U to toggle the checkbox. ([LocalKinAI/kinclaw](https://github.com/LocalKinAI/kinclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/notes_checklist) · ★9)
- **notes_export_pdf**: Export a Notes note to a PDF file at an absolute path. Notes doesn't expose Export-as-PDF via AppleScript dictionary; this skill drives the File menu + Save sheet via UI scripting. The Save sheet is the standard NSSavePanel — we type the filename into the focused field, use Cmd+Shift+G to set the destination directory, then press Return to commit. The skill does NOT use the Print → Save as PDF route (3+ extra dialog clicks); it uses Notes' direct File → Export as PDF... menu. After the save, the skill polls for the file's existence to confirm completion before returning. ([LocalKinAI/kinclaw](https://github.com/LocalKinAI/kinclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/notes_export_pdf) · ★9)
- **notes_format**: Apply a Notes-app text format (bold / italic / underline / title / heading / subheading / body) to text in a note. Handles selection + format keystroke + verification. The #1 mistake agents make: pressing Cmd+B without selecting text first. Notes' format shortcuts only act on the current selection; no selection = no-op. This skill always selects first. ([LocalKinAI/kinclaw](https://github.com/LocalKinAI/kinclaw/tree/HEAD/skills/notes_format) · ★9)
